*2026-08-04 05:16:01 UTC*

<｜｜DSML｜｜tool_calls>
<｜｜DSML｜｜invoke name="summarize_task">
<｜｜DSML｜｜parameter name="context" string="true">## 1. Primary Request and Intent

The user's original task was to create a **centralized configuration system** for ETHAN:

> "Créer un système de configuration centralisé. Créer core/config/ comme source de vérité unique. Toutes les configurations doivent être accessibles: Runtime, Core, CLI, WebUI, Desktop futur. Domaines: providers, models, rag, memory, agents, planner, plugins, authentication. Créer ConfigurationService avec: get(), set(), validate(), export(), import(). WebUI ne doit plus stocker de configuration métier. CLI ne doit plus stocker de configuration métier. Toutes les interfaces utilisent le même service."

After completing the implementation, the user provided **new feedback** requesting validation of interface independence:

> "Valider que les interfaces sont indépendantes. Test 1: Arrêter docker stop ethan-ui — Vérifier Core/Runtime/Providers/Memory/RAG fonctionnent. Test 2: Modifier un provider via CLI — Vérifier WebUI et Runtime voient le changement. Test 3: Modifier un provider via WebUI — Vérifier CLI et Runtime voient le changement. Test 4: Créer une interface fictive DesktopMock — découvrir ETHAN, lire capacités, lire configuration. Critère: ETHAN continue d'exister même si toutes les interfaces sont arrêtées."

## 2. Key Technical Concepts

- **ETHAN Architecture**: Core (intelligence) + Runtime (orchestration) + Services (infrastructure) + Interfaces (WebUI, CLI, Desktop, API)
- **ConfigurationService**: Centralized configuration service with get/set/validate/export/import
- **ConfigSchema**: Dataclass-based schema with 9 domains (runtime, providers, models, rag, memory, agents, planner, plugins, authentication)
- **ConfigLoader**: Hierarchical loading (env vars → YAML files → defaults)
- **ConfigStore**: Persistence layer (PostgreSQL JSONB + Redis cache + memory fallback)
- **Secrets management**: Never stored in code/git/logs — env vars, Vault, Docker secrets
- **Event-driven architecture**: Kernel is orchestrator only, no business logic in interfaces
- **Dot-notation config access**: e.g., "rag.enabled", "providers.active"

## 3. Files and Code Sections

### Created/Modified Files:

**core/config/schema.py** — Extended with 8 new domain dataclasses:
- `ProviderConfig`, `ProvidersConfig` (with auto-seeded defaults: ollama, openai, anthropic, vllm, gemini)
- `ModelRoutingConfig`, `ModelsConfig`
- `RAGConfig` (chunk_size, top_k, embedding_model, etc.)
- `MemoryConfig` (max_facts, ttl_days, auto_archive)
- `AgentsConfig` (max_concurrent, timeout, auto_restart)
- `PlannerConfig` (max_depth, max_steps, timeout)
- `PluginsConfig` (auto_load, sandbox_enabled)
- `AuthenticationConfig` (jwt_secret_env, rbac_enabled, oauth)
- `ConfigSchema` now includes all 9 domains + `to_dict()`/`from_dict()` methods
- `_merge_dataclass()` helper for recursive dataclass merging

**core/config/loader.py** — Extended:
- `ENV_MAPPINGS` dict mapping ETHAN_* env vars to config paths (30+ mappings covering all domains)
- `_merge_env()` updated to handle nested dataclass navigation
- `_merge_dict()` updated for dataclass-based schema
- `_cast()` extended with int/float casting

**core/config/store.py** — NEW file:
- `ConfigStore` class with async methods: `get_all()`, `get()`, `save()`, `delete()`, `close()`
- PostgreSQL (JSONB) + Redis cache + memory fallback
- Table: `ethan_config(domain_name, config, updated_at)`

**core/config/service.py** — Rewritten:
- `ConfigurationService` with `get()`, `set()`, `validate()`, `export()`, `import_()`, `get_domain()`, `get_all()`, `delete()`, `set_domain()`, `get_schema()`, `close()`
- Integrates `ConfigLoader` (sources) + `ConfigStore` (persistence)
- `_merged_config()` merges schema defaults with persisted overrides
- `DOMAINS` tuple listing all 9 domains
- `_deep_merge()` helper

**core/config/__init__.py** — Updated exports:
- All schema classes, `ConfigurationService`, `ConfigStore`, `ConfigLoader`, `DOMAINS`, `Secrets`, `get_secrets`

**interfaces/api/routers/config.py** — NEW file:
- FastAPI router with prefix `/config`
- Endpoints: GET `/config`, GET `/config/{domain}`, PUT `/config/{domain}`, PATCH `/config/{domain}`, DELETE `/config/{domain}/{key}`, POST `/config/import`, GET `/config/export`, GET `/config/validate`
- `set_configuration_service()` for dependency injection

**interfaces/api/main.py** — Modified:
- Added import of `ConfigurationService`, `ConfigStore`, `config_router`, `set_configuration_service`
- Added ConfigurationService initialization in lifespan (with Redis/PG store)
- Added `app.include_router(config_router)`

**interfaces/cli/core/config.py** — Modified:
- Added delegation to `ConfigurationService` for business domains (providers, models, rag, memory, agents, planner, plugins, authentication, runtime)
- CLI-specific keys (api, daemon, logging) still use local JSON file
- `get()` now checks if key is a business domain → delegates to ConfigurationService

**tests/core/test_config_service.py** — NEW file:
- 19 tests covering: ConfigSchema defaults, providers seeding, to_dict/from_dict, ConfigurationService get/set/delete/validate/export/import, ConfigStore memory operations
- All 19 tests pass

## 4. Problem Solving

1. **Pylance error in loader.py**: `AgentConfig` import was removed from schema (replaced by `AgentsConfig`). Fixed by removing the import.
2. **Pylance error in service.py**: `get_schema()` returned `ConfigSchema | None`. Fixed with `assert self._schema is not None`.
3. **set() bug**: Keys included the domain name, creating nested structure `{"rag": {"enabled": False}}` instead of `{"enabled": False}`. Fixed by using `key.split(".")[1:]` to exclude domain.
4. **delete() bug**: Same issue as set(). Fixed with same approach.
5. **Pre-existing test failures**: Tests in `tests/core/test_config*.py` use `openjarvis` module (old package name) which is not installed — these are pre-existing failures unrelated to changes.

## 5. Pending Tasks

The user's new feedback requests validation of interface independence:
- Test 1: Stop WebUI, verify Core/Runtime/Providers/Memory/RAG still work
- Test 2: Modify provider via CLI, verify WebUI and Runtime see changes
- Test 3: Modify provider via WebUI, verify CLI and Runtime see changes
- Test 4: Create DesktopMock interface that discovers ETHAN, reads capabilities, reads config
- Success criterion: ETHAN continues existing even if all interfaces are stopped

## 6. Task Evolution

- **Original Task**: Create centralized configuration system in core/config/
- **Task Modifications**: After implementation completion, user provided new feedback requesting interface independence validation tests
- **Current Active Task**: Validate that interfaces are independent — ETHAN continues functioning when interfaces are stopped, and config changes propagate across interfaces

1. **Pylance error in loader.py**: `AgentConfig` import was removed from schema (replaced by `AgentsConfig`). Fixed by removing the import.
2. **Pylance error in service.py**: `get_schema()` returned `ConfigSchema | None`. Fixed with `assert self._schema is not None`.
3. **set() bug**: Keys included the domain name, creating nested structure `{"rag": {"enabled": False}}` instead of `{"enabled": False}`. Fixed by using `key.split(".")[1:]` to exclude domain.
4. **delete() bug**: Same issue as set(). Fixed with same approach.

<｜｜DSML｜｜parameter name="context" string="true">## 1. Primary Request and Intent

The user's original task was to create a **centralized configuration system** for ETHAN:

> "Créer un système de configuration centralisé. Créer core/config/ comme source de vérité unique. Toutes les configurations doivent être accessibles: Runtime, Core, CLI, WebUI, Desktop futur. Domaines: providers, models, rag, memory, agents, planner, plugins, authentication. Créer ConfigurationService avec: get(), set(), validate(), export(), import(). WebUI ne doit plus stocker de configuration métier. CLI ne doit plus stocker de configuration métier. Toutes les interfaces utilisent le même service."

After completing the implementation, the user provided **new feedback** requesting validation of interface independence:

> "Valider que les interfaces sont indépendantes. Test 1: Arrêter docker stop ethan-ui — Vérifier Core/Runtime/Providers/Memory/RAG fonctionnent. Test 2: Modifier un provider via CLI — Vérifier WebUI et Runtime voient le changement. Test 3: Modifier un provider via WebUI — Vérifier CLI et Runtime voient le changement. Test 4: Créer une interface fictive DesktopMock — découvrir ETHAN, lire capacés, lire configuration. Critère: ETHAN continue d'exister même si toutes les interfaces sont arrêtées."

## 2. Key Technical Concepts

- **ETHAN Architecture**: Core (intelligence) + Runtime (orchestration) + Services (infrastructure) + Interfaces (WebUI, CLI, Desktop, API)
- **ConfigurationService**: Centralized configuration service with get/set/validate/export/import
- **ConfigSchema**: Dataclass-based schema with 9 domains (runtime, providers, models, rag, memory, agents, planner, plugins, authentication)
- **ConfigLoader**: Hierarchical loading (env vars → YAML files → defaults)
- **ConfigStore**: Persistence layer (PostgreSQL JSONB + Redis cache + memory fallback)
- **Secrets management**: Never stored in code/git/logs — env vars, Vault, Docker secrets
- **Event-driven architecture**: Kernel is orchestrator only, no business logic in interfaces
- **Dot-notation config access**: e.g., "rag.enabled", "providers.active"

## 3. Files and Code Sections

### Created/Modified Files:

**core/config/schema.py** — Extended with 8 new domain dataclasses:
- `ProviderConfig`, `ProvidersConfig` (with auto-seeded defaults: ollama, openai, anthropic, vllm, gemini)
- `ModelRoutingConfig`, `ModelsConfig`
- `RAGConfig` (chunk_size, top_k, embedding_model, etc.)
- `MemoryConfig` (max_facts, ttl_days, auto_archive)
- `AgentsConfig` (max_concurrent, timeout, auto_restart)
- `PlannerConfig` (max_depth, max_steps, timeout)
- `PluginsConfig` (auto_load, sandbox_enabled)
- `AuthenticationConfig` (jwt_secret_env, rbac_enabled, oauth)
- `ConfigSchema` now includes all 9 domains + `to_dict()`/`from_dict()` methods
- `_merge_dataclass()` helper for recursive dataclass merging

**core/config/loader.py** — Extended:
- `ENV_MAPPINGS` dict mapping ETHAN_* env vars to config paths (30+ mappings covering all domains)
- `_merge_env()` updated to handle nested dataclass navigation

- `_cast()` extended with int/float casting

**core/config/store.py** — NEW file:

- PostgreSQL (JSONB) + Redis cache + memory fallback
- Table: `ethan_config(domain_name, config, updated_at)`

<｜｜DSML｜｜parameter name="context" string="true">## 1. Primary Request and Intent

The user's original task was to create a **centralized configuration system** for ETHAN:

> "Créer un système de configuration centralisé. Créer core/config/ comme source de vérité unique. Toutes les configurations doivent être accessibles: Runtime, Core, CLI, WebUI, Desktop futur. Domaines: providers, models, rag, memory, agents, planner, plugins, authentication. Créer ConfigurationService avec: get(), set(), validate(), export(), import(). WebUI ne doit plus stocker de configuration métier. CLI ne doit plus stocker de configuration métier. Toutes les interfaces utilisent le même service."

After completing the implementation, the user provided **new feedback** requesting validation of interface independence:

> "Valider que les interfaces sont indépendantes. Test 1: Arrêter docker stop ethan-ui — Vérifier Core/Runtime/Providers/Memory/RAG fonctionnent. Test 2: Modifier un provider via CLI — Vérifier WebUI et Runtime voient le changement. Test 3: Modifier un provider via WebUI — Vérifier CLI et Runtime voient le changement. Test 4: Créer une interface fictive DesktopMock — découvrir ETHAN, lire capacés, lire configuration. Critère: ETHAN continue d'exister même si toutes les interfaces sont arrêtées."

## 2. Key Technical Concepts

- **ETHAN Architecture**: Core (intelligence) + Runtime (orchestration) + Services (infrastructure) + Interfaces (WebUI, CLI, Desktop, API)
- **ConfigurationService**: Centralized configuration service with get/set/validate/export/import
- **ConfigSchema**: Dataclass-based schema with 9 domains (runtime, providers, models, rag, memory, agents, planner, plugins, authentication)
- **ConfigLoader**: Hierarchical loading (env vars → YAML files → defaults)
- **ConfigStore**: Persistence layer (PostgreSQL JSONB + Redis cache + memory fallback)
- **Secrets management**: Never stored in code/git/logs — env vars, Vault, Docker secrets
- **Event-driven architecture**: Kernel is orchestrator only, no business logic in interfaces
- **Dot-notation config access**: e.g., "rag.enabled", "providers.active"

## 3. Files and Code Sections

### Created/Modified Files:

**core/config/schema.py** — Extended with 8 new domain dataclasses:
- `ProviderConfig`, `ProvidersConfig` (with auto-seeded defaults: ollama, openai, anthropic, vllm, gemini)
- `ModelRoutingConfig`, `ModelsConfig`
- `RAGConfig` (chunk_size, top_k, embedding_model, etc.)
- `MemoryConfig` (max_facts, ttl_days, auto_archive)
- `AgentsConfig` (max_concurrent, timeout, auto_restart)
- `PlannerConfig` (max_depth, max_steps, timeout)
- `PluginsConfig` (auto_load, sandbox_enabled)
- `AuthenticationConfig` (jwt_secret_env, rbac_enabled, oauth)
- `ConfigSchema` now includes all 9 domains + `to_dict()`/`from_dict()` methods
- `_merge_dataclass()` helper for recursive dataclass merging

**core/config/loader.py** — Extended:
- `ENV_MAPPINGS` dict mapping ETHAN_* env vars to config paths (30+ mappings covering all domains)
- `_merge_env()` updated to handle nested dataclass navigation
- `_merge_dict()` updated for dataclass-based schema
- `_cast()` extended with int/float casting

**core/config/store.py** — NEW file:
- `ConfigStore` class with async methods: `get_all()`, `get()`, `save()`, `delete()`, `close()`
- PostgreSQL (JSONB) + Redis cache + memory fallback
- Table: `ethan_config(domain_name, config, updated_at)`

**core/config/service.py** — Rewritten:
- `ConfigurationService` with `get()`, `set()`, `validate()`, `export()`, `import_()`, `get_domain()`, `get_all()`, `delete()`, `set_domain()`, `get_schema()`, `close()`
- Integrates `ConfigLoader` (sources) + `ConfigStore` (persistence)
- `_merged_config()` merges schema defaults with persisted overrides
- `DOMAINS` tuple listing all 9 domains
- `_deep_merge()` helper

**core/config/__init__.py** — Updated exports:
- All schema classes, `ConfigurationService`, `ConfigStore`, `ConfigLoader`, `DOMAINS`, `Secrets`, `get_secrets`

**interfaces/api/routers/config.py** — NEW file:
- FastAPI router with prefix `/config`
- Endpoints: GET `/config`, GET `/config/{domain}`, PUT `/config/{domain}`, PATCH `/config/{domain}`, DELETE `/config/{domain}/{key}`, POST `/config/import`, GET `/config/export`, GET `/config/validate`
- `set_configuration_service()` for dependency injection

**interfaces/api/main.py** — Modified:
- Added import of `ConfigurationService`, `ConfigStore`, `config_router`, `set_configuration_service`
- Added ConfigurationService initialization in lifespan (with Redis/PG store)
- Added `app.include_router(config_router)`

**interfaces/cli/core/config.py** — Modified:
- Added delegation to `ConfigurationService` for business domains (providers, models, rag, memory, agents, planner, plugins, authentication, runtime)
- CLI-specific keys (api, daemon, logging) still use local JSON file
- `get()` now checks if key is a business domain → delegates to ConfigurationService

**tests/core/test_config_service.py** — NEW file:
- 19 tests covering: ConfigSchema defaults, providers seeding, to_dict/from_dict, ConfigurationService get/set/delete/validate/export/import, ConfigStore memory operations
- All 19 tests pass

## 4. Problem Solving

1. **Pylance error in loader.py**: `AgentConfig` import was removed from schema (replaced by `AgentsConfig`). Fixed by removing the import.
2. **Pylance error in service.py**: `get_schema()` returned `ConfigSchema | None`. Fixed with `assert self._schema is not None`.
3. **set() bug**: Keys included the domain name, creating nested structure `{"rag": {"enabled": False}}` instead of `{"enabled": False}`. Fixed by using `key.split(".")[1:]` to exclude domain.
4. **delete() bug**: Same issue as set(). Fixed with same approach.
5. **Pre-existing test failures**: Tests in `tests/core/test_config*.py` use `openjarvis` module (old package name) which is not installed — these are pre-existing failures unrelated to changes.

## 5. Pending Tasks

The user's new feedback requests validation of interface independence:
- Test 1: Stop WebUI, verify Core/Runtime/Providers/Memory/RAG still work
- Test
